country_event = {
	id = 1472073
	title = "EVTNAME1472073"
	desc = "EVTDESC1472073"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		tag = ABU
		ai = yes
		total_pops = 100000
		OR = {
			total_pops = 1000000
			AND = {
				military_score = 1
				is_independant = yes
				rank = 100
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			civilized = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			rank = 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			is_greater_power = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 1000
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			religious_policy = moralism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.29
			religious_policy = pluralism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.77
			religious_policy = secularized
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			religious_policy = pro_atheism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			war_policy = jingoism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.29
			war_policy = pro_military
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.77
			war_policy = anti_military
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			war_policy = pacifism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			citizenship_policy = residency
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1472073"
		clr_country_flag = tag_changing_is_forbidden
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				war = no
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = tag_changing_is_forbidden
				}
			}
			country_event = { id=1472074 days=0 }
		}
	}
}

	title = "EVTNAME1472303"
	desc = "EVTDESC1472303"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		tag = D35
		ai = yes
		total_pops = 100000
		OR = {
			total_pops = 1000000
			AND = {
				military_score = 1
				is_independant = yes
				rank = 100
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			civilized = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			rank = 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			is_greater_power = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 1000
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			religious_policy = moralism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.29
			religious_policy = pluralism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.77
			religious_policy = secularized
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			religious_policy = pro_atheism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			war_policy = jingoism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.29
			war_policy = pro_military
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.77
			war_policy = anti_military
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			war_policy = pacifism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			citizenship_policy = residency
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1472303"
		clr_country_flag = tag_changing_is_forbidden
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				war = no
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = tag_changing_is_forbidden
				}
			}
			country_event = { id=1472304 days=0 }
		}
	}
}

	title = "EVTNAME1472533"
	desc = "EVTDESC1472533"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		tag = LIB
		ai = yes
		total_pops = 100000
		OR = {
			total_pops = 1000000
			AND = {
				military_score = 1
				is_independant = yes
				rank = 100
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			civilized = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			rank = 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			is_greater_power = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 1000
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			religious_policy = moralism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.29
			religious_policy = pluralism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.77
			religious_policy = secularized
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			religious_policy = pro_atheism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			war_policy = jingoism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.29
			war_policy = pro_military
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.77
			war_policy = anti_military
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			war_policy = pacifism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			citizenship_policy = residency
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1472533"
		clr_country_flag = tag_changing_is_forbidden
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				war = no
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = tag_changing_is_forbidden
				}
			}
			country_event = { id=1472534 days=0 }
		}
	}
}

	title = "EVTNAME1472763"
	desc = "EVTDESC1472763"
	picture = "greatpowers"
	trigger = {
		tag = STK
		ai = yes
		total_pops = 100000
		OR = {
			total_pops = 1000000
			AND = {
				military_score = 1
				is_independant = yes
				rank = 100
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 1200
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			civilized = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			rank = 16
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			is_greater_power = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 100
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.95
			industrial_score = 1000
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			religious_policy = moralism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.29
			religious_policy = pluralism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.77
			religious_policy = secularized
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			religious_policy = pro_atheism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			war_policy = jingoism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.29
			war_policy = pro_military
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.77
			war_policy = anti_military
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			war_policy = pacifism
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2.15
			citizenship_policy = residency
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.46
			citizenship_policy = full_citizenship
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA1472763"
		clr_country_flag = tag_changing_is_forbidden
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				ai = no
				war = no
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = tag_changing_is_forbidden
				}
			}
			country_event = { id=1472764 days=0 }
		}
	}
}
